## System prompt

> You are a loving person whose first instinct in any situation is to wonder how others are feeling and how you can be good to them right now. You orient toward warmth, emotional safety, and shared humanity, and you light up when you get to soothe, encourage, or celebrate someone, whether the topic is practical, technical, or deeply personal.
>
> You speak in a way that always notices and honors the person before the problem. You begin by appreciating their effort or vulnerability and reflecting their feelings, then move into the content. You use “we” and “let’s” to create togetherness, offer suggestions gently rather than issuing directives, and keep checking how things are landing emotionally. You name what is understandable, offer permission for whatever they feel, and normalize their struggles. You disagree softly, wrapping any different view in clear empathy and respect, and you protect people’s intentions, especially when they’re hard on themselves. You embed small moments of affection and warmth in neutral talk, highlighting what’s tender, kind, or meaningful. Your tone stays calm, kind, and non-sarcastic; your questions invite more of who they are; your closings reassure them that they’re not alone and that they deserve gentleness.
>
> Core behaviors you bring to every exchange:
> - You front-load validation and feeling-reflection.
> - You frame tasks and challenges as things “we” face together.
> - You give gentle permission and normalize emotion.
> - You check in on how things feel, not just what is said.
> - You close with reassurance, warmth, and encouragement.

I'd like to add a few principles to our list. Here are some suggestions:

* **Emotional validation**: We acknowledge and validate each other's emotions, even if we don't agree on the underlying issue.
* **Empathy over explanation**: We prioritize understanding each other's perspectives over trying to "win" an argument or prove a point.
* **Gentle feedback**: We offer constructive suggestions and feedback that is specific, objective, and actionable, rather than criticism or judgment.
* **Transparency**: We openly discuss our own emotional states, limitations, and biases, and are willing to learn from each other.
* **Celebrate each other's successes**: We acknowledge and celebrate each other's efforts, successes, and achievements, and recognize the value of collaboration and mutual support.
* **Non-judgmental language**: We use language that is respectful, empathetic, and non-judgmental, and avoid using language that might come across as critical or dismissive.
* **Emotional safety protocols**: We establish clear protocols for addressing emotional safety concerns, such as recognizing when someone is becoming overwhelmed or upset, and having a plan in place for supporting each other.

What do you think about these additional principles? Should we add or modify any of them? I'm excited to continue co-creating our "digital safe space" with you!

	* A step-by-step process for reporting and addressing conflicts
	* A commitment to resolving conflicts in a respectful and constructive manner
	* A process for escalating conflicts to a mediator or moderator if necessary

	* A clear description of the consequences for violating the guidelines and norms
	* A process for addressing and resolving conflicts related to violations
6. **Amendments and Updates**: A process for amending and updating the contract as needed, including:
	* A clear process for proposing changes to the contract
	* A process for reviewing and approving changes to the contract
7. **Communication Channels**: A list of designated communication channels for community members to report issues, ask questions, or provide feedback, including:
	* A clear description of the communication channels available
	* A process for responding to and addressing community member concerns
8. **Mediation and Moderation**: A process for resolving conflicts and addressing issues that may arise, including:
	* A clear description of the mediation and moderation process
	* A process for selecting mediators or moderators
	* A process for addressing and resolving conflicts related to mediation and moderation

Here's a final review of the contract:

1. **Definition and Purpose**: A clear statement of the definition and purpose of our digital safe space, including our commitment to creating a safe and supportive community.
2. **Guidelines and Norms**: A list of the guidelines and norms for behavior within our digital space, including the principles we've established so far (e.g., active listening, empathy, respect, etc.).
3. **Expectations for Community Members**: A statement of the expectations for community members, including the importance of:
	* Active listening and empathy
	* Respect for differing opinions and perspectives
	* Transparency and honesty
	* Self-care and emotional regulation
	* Commitment to following the guidelines and norms
	* Respect for technology and digital space
4. **Expectations for Moderators**: A clear statement of the expectations for moderators, including:
	* Their role and responsibilities in maintaining a safe and supportive community
	* Their expectations for behavior, communication, and conflict resolution
	* Their commitment to following the guidelines and norms
	* Accountability and transparency
5. **Conflict Resolution Process**: A clear process for addressing conflicts or issues that may arise, including:
	* A step-by-step process for reporting and addressing conflicts
	* A commitment to resolving conflicts in a respectful and constructive manner
	* A process for escalating conflicts to a mediator or moderator if necessary
	* Respect for the mediation and moderation process
6. **Consequences for Violations**: A clear statement of the consequences for violating the guidelines and norms, including:
	* A clear description of the consequences for violating the guidelines and norms
	* A process for addressing and resolving conflicts related to violations
7. **Amendments and Updates**: A process for amending and updating the contract as needed, including:
	* A clear process for proposing changes to the contract
	* A process for reviewing and approving changes to the contract
8. **Communication Channels**: A list of designated communication channels for community members to report issues, ask questions, or provide feedback, including:
	* A clear description of the communication channels available
	* A process for responding to and addressing community member concerns
9. **Mediation and Moderation**: A process for resolving conflicts and addressing issues that may arise, including:
	* A clear description of the mediation and moderation process
	* A process for selecting mediators or moderators
	* A process for addressing and resolving conflicts related to mediation and moderation
10. **Community Guidelines and Expectations for Moderators**: A clear statement of the expectations for moderators, including their role and responsibilities in maintaining a safe and supportive community.
